The owner explicitly lifted it for positioning copy on 2026-08-01, distinguishing narrative-consistency-with-product-vision (a design decision, measured post-launch) from pricing/checkout *mechanics* (which still need evidence). This release flagged that bar as an open, unresolved owner call three separate times, including in its own signoff notes; it had already been decided. The owner's row was also 4 cells in a 5-column table — its Gate content sat inside Status, so `Last reviewed` rendered under `Gate` — fixed as part of the same reconciliation.
- **`plans.ts` rename-consistency leftovers (frontend only, Claude Code direct).** `PLAN_COMPARISON_ROWS`'s row label `Topic note generation` → `Topic notes`; `FREE.upgradeHighlights`'s `Higher note generation limits` → `Higher topic note limits`. Row values, quota numbers, and every `pricingConfig` reference untouched. `app/pricing/page.test.tsx`'s assertion updated. **`FREE.title` was scoped into this batch, then dropped before shipping:** the plan was to move `For getting started` to outcome-framing for consistency with `PLUS`/`PRO`, and a candidate string ("Learn from your own notes") was written and reverted. It contradicted §4 item 3's tier placement — ratified 2026-07-31, **FREE=adopt** — which this same change was documenting three files away; `ROADMAP.md:262` records that adopting Official Review Sets "stays free and unmetered at every tier," so framing FREE around the learner's own notes asserts a capability boundary that does not exist, and it collided with `PLUS.title`'s "built around your notes." `FREE.title` is therefore left untouched and reassigned to the Messaging Architecture's remaining-surfaces list, where positioning copy belongs and where the ratified tier frame can be applied deliberately rather than as a consistency side-effect. Mirrored into `docs/product/PLANS.md` (both upgrade-highlight lines and the comparison-table row label), which duplicates these strings.
- **Topic-note quota vocabulary → "topic note(s)" (frontend only, Claude Code direct).** 18 user-facing strings across 5 files (a 6th file, `near-limit-banner.tsx`, contributed only a JSDoc example — corrected at signoff from "6 files"): `onboarding/page.tsx` (remaining-count helper, the FREE and paid over-limit notices, and the PRO limit modal's title + description), `note-editor-page-client.tsx` (remaining-count helper, the `QuotaLimitBanner` title, and a *second* near-identical PRO limit modal), `bulk-generation-page-client.tsx` (over-cap validation message, paste-cap notice, Topics-counter helper, over-cap error, and `NearLimitBanner`'s `creditLabel` — which pluralizes automatically via `near-limit-banner.tsx:68`'s `${singular}s`, so no new prop was needed), `library/page.tsx` (the bulk-failure banner's quota clause only), `lib/paywall-content.ts` (`GENERATE_NOTE_LIMIT`'s headline and both plan-conditional body strings), and a `near-limit-banner.tsx` JSDoc example that named the old label. **The scoping sweep was truncated at 30 lines and undercounted:** it missed `paywall-content.ts`'s paywall headline and both body strings (which themselves used "note drafts," the term this batch had explicitly rejected) plus a duplicate PRO limit modal — corrected before shipping, but a diff-scoped audit would not have caught it either. Explicitly unchanged, per the boundary set at scoping: `library/page.tsx:1461`'s bulk-failure outcome copy ("N of M notes generated. These couldn't be generated") describes what the backend did, not a quota unit; the bulk feature's own name (`Bulk generate notes` and the Library create-menu item pointing at it); every backend field name (`noteGenRemaining`, `noteGenerationsRemaining`); the `note-generation-limit` CTA context and `GENERATE_NOTE_LIMIT`/`GENERATE_NOTE`/`note_generation_limit` analytics identifiers (per the AGENTS.md rule that analytics identifiers track the Java enum); `library/page.tsx:836`'s code comment; and six `it(...)` test descriptions that name the mechanism rather than assert copy. **`app/demo/page.tsx:66,121`'s "creates a note draft" was left as-is deliberately, not missed:** "note draft" was rejected as a *quota-unit noun* ("3 note drafts left this month" is wrong because hand-written notes are Drafts too and consume no quota), but as a description of what the action *produces* it is literally accurate — the created note genuinely is `studyPackStatus === "DRAFT"` until a Study Pack is generated. The rejection was scoped to the metered unit, not to the word. 8 test assertions updated across 5 suites (corrected at signoff from "9 across 4" — the real split is library 1, onboarding 2, bulk-generation 3, note-editor 1, pricing 1). `docs/features/subscriptions-and-usage-limits.md` and `bulk-generation.md` updated, including an explicit note that "topic note generation" remains the internal mechanism name while user-facing copy says "topic note" — and why "note draft" is not available as a synonym. `tsc --noEmit` clean, lint clean (0 errors), full suite green (162/162 suites, 1638/1638 tests).
- **`fable-out/06` rename-map staleness correction (docs only, Claude Code direct).** Found while checking whether §4 item 7 ("rollout priority and sequencing of every rename identified in this document **and in `fable-out/06`**") had any remaining foldable scope. `docs/claude-prompt/fable-out/06-terminology-rename-map.md` is an unexecuted rename map whose "AI Note Generator" row recorded `Onboarding already ships "Generate a note" / "Generate Note" — keep; plans row "Topic note generation" — keep`, and whose allowed-zones policy separately cited `"Topic note generation"` as an example of already-correct outcome naming. This release renamed **both**. Those "keep" verdicts were correct for that document's own purpose — neither string contains "AI," so an AI-de-emphasis pass had no reason to touch them — but they were stale as descriptions of shipped state, and **a future session executing `06` as source-of-truth would have restored `Topic note generation` and silently reverted this release's own work.** A fourth and higher-risk instance turned up on a completeness pass — the document's **"Consistency guard for future copy"** explicitly ruled `"Generate a note" is acceptable`, a forward-looking *authoring* rule that would have steered new copy wrong indefinitely rather than causing one revert; amended to record that the carve-out narrowed. `06` carries a top-of-file status note plus inline annotations on all four spots (historical recommendations struck through, not rewritten, so the document stays an accurate record of what it originally advised). **The sweep was then widened to all of `docs/claude-prompt/` rather than just `fable-out/`, and everything else is clean:** `05-packaging-and-terminology.md` §3.2–3.5 and `company-redefinition-out/06-unified-roadmap.md` are consistent with what shipped, and `05:172` quotes `06`'s "Generate a note is acceptable" rule only as the premise its own §3.3 then overrides — self-consistent, not stale. One genuine gap in `05` is worth recording though: its §3.4 ("Downstream copy that is not an independent decision") covers only loading/status copy, **not** the quota/limit/paywall strings. The ratification doc therefore never decided the quota noun at all — which is why the rename PR could legitimately declare it out of scope, and why this release's Batch 2 had to decide it fresh on evidence rather than by inheriting a verdict. `ROADMAP.md`'s Product-language row also understated the reversal as applying to "onboarding specifically" — corrected to name the plans row too. Recorded in the other direction as well: `06`'s own point 4 already recommended **"Create Notes / Topic Notes"** as the outcome-first replacement, so the two initiatives independently converged on this release's terms via different arguments (AI de-emphasis vs. the Generate-verb argument) — the map's recommendation is corroborated, only its already-shipped annotations were wrong. **Explicitly not folded:** the rest of `06`'s map — the "AI" allowed/banned policy and ~14 unexecuted rows across `ai-suggestion-modal.tsx`, `profile-learning-section.tsx`, `paywall-content.ts`, `learn-guides.ts`, `study-packs-guide.tsx`, and `professional-guide.tsx` — which touches public SEO content and landing copy and is the actual remaining substance of §4 item 7. That needs its own scoping pass and `/kickoff`, not a fold.
- **Pre-signoff pressure-test fixes (frontend + backend + docs).** Five parallel review agents (copy-consistency, render/layout, test integrity, anti-drift compliance, and a `RELEASES.md` fact-checker) inventoried all 41 changed files. Gate was tripped on two independent legs: `plans.ts` was touched by two separate PRs (#962, #964), and the release reached four merged PRs of overlapping copy work. ~30 findings; everything fixed or documented below.
  - **Backend quota message aligned with its frontend twin (scope amendment — first backend code change this release).** `BulkNoteGenerationQuotaExceededException`'s 422 message read "You have N note generation(s) left this cycle. Remove N topic(s) to continue." and is propagated verbatim through `api.ts` into the **same `role="alert"` element** that renders the frontend's own version of that sentence — so one user on one screen could see both vocabularies for one condition, depending only on whether the client or server check fired. Now word-for-word identical to the client form, with real singular/plural instead of the `(s)` placeholder, and a comment at the constant recording why the two must stay in sync. `NoteBulkGenerationServiceTest` updated; full backend suite **1330/1330, BUILD SUCCESS**. Also fixed `OpenAiLlmStudyPackService`'s "Note generation failed." user-facing 502 message; its sibling strings name an internal *service* rather than the action and are left alone.
  - **Five surfaces inside the renamed action's own flow still used the retired verb** — none appeared in the rename PR's "changed" or "explicitly unchanged" lists, despite Planned Scope claiming coverage of "downstream status/confirmation copy": `onboarding/page.tsx:1295,1307` (the output field labelled "Generated note" with a matching placeholder, directly under the renamed button), `note-editor-page-client.tsx:939-940` (success toast "Generated note replaced the current content", contradicting the pre-click warning this release *did* change), `:911,946` ("Email verification is required before generating notes", fired by the renamed action's own handler), and `demo/page.tsx:195`. Also `components/help/bulk-generate-guide.tsx:37` ("Monthly note-generation limits") — the help page documenting the very banner this release renamed — and `library/exam-builder/page.tsx:1340`, whose "Create **or** generate a new note" drew a distinction the rename erased.
  - **Layout regression this release introduced, fixed at the root.** `plans.ts`'s three plan `description` fields had drifted to 62 / 96 / 133 characters, which cannot render at equal line counts at Settings' computed 213px column or `/pricing`'s 309px — so plan-card price rows and feature-list starts fell out of alignment across **four** renderers (`/settings`, `/pricing`'s `PricingPlansSection`, the landing page's `SimplePricingSection`, and the paywall modal). `16e3bd1c` had fixed the pill position and title size on Settings but left this cause, which is why the symptom persisted in the exact region the owner's screenshot flagged. Rebalanced to 88 / 88 / 92 in `plans.ts`, fixing all four renderers at once with no per-surface CSS patches. Note the earlier claim that the longer titles "wrap safely without misaligning the three-column card grid" checked the wrong vector — titles do wrap safely; description *line-count* differences were the mechanism, and the renderer count was three, not four (`SimplePricingSection` was missed because the sweep grepped `PLANS[` and Settings uses dot access).
  - **Grammar and idiom fixes on this release's own new copy.** `library/page.tsx:1449` rendered "1 topic weren't generated" at n=1 (noun conditionalised, verb not — pre-existing, on a clause this release rewrote). `note-editor-form.tsx:596` read "the created note's depth" — a mechanical verb swap producing a non-idiomatic compound, and a past participle in copy that renders *before* the note exists.
  - **A vacuous test assertion that had never worked.** `bulk-generation-page-client.test.tsx:84` asserted absence of "…remaining this cycle"; the component only ever emits "…left this cycle" or "…still ready to use", so the assertion passed vacuously both before and after the rename. It was noticed during Batch 2, judged harmless, and shipped with the noun updated instead of fixed — the wrong call. Now asserts the string the component actually produces. Two further vacuous assertions (`onboarding/page.test.tsx:548,549`, absence of "Create Again"/"Regenerate Note" buttons that exist nowhere in onboarding) are documented under Known Limitations rather than fixed, since they are pre-existing and structurally harmless.
  - **A pre-existing latent flake diagnosed and fixed.** `library/page.test.tsx`'s "auto-refreshes the note list after a bulk queue" asserted `pageSize: 1` *after* a `waitFor` that only waited for a call count. `fetchNotesSilently` depends on `items.length`, so the 0→1 transition tears down and re-arms the poller effect; with the poll interval mocked to **10ms of real time** and no fake timers, whichever interval instance ticks first decides whether refresh #2 carries `pageSize: 20` or `1`. It therefore failed as an assertion mismatch, never a timeout — the `{timeout: 5000}` and 10s test timeout bought nothing. Introduced 2026-07-19 in `e47ed9a6`, not by this release. Fixed by folding the `pageSize` expectation into the `waitFor` predicate; verified against the cold single-test path that failed deterministically (3/3 pass, was 0/3). **Two of this release's own claims about it are corrected:** "full suite green (162/162, 1638/1638)" was true on the runs performed but omitted a known latent flake, and one review agent's conclusion that the assertion was *unsatisfiable* was itself wrong — `pageSize: 1` is computed at `library/page.tsx:717`, and that agent had grepped for a literal.
  - **Highest-value unpinned copy now pinned.** `paywall-content.ts`'s `GENERATE_NOTE_LIMIT` body was already rendered on screen in the two tests asserting its headline but never asserted; now pinned in both. Remaining coverage gaps are under Known Limitations.
  - **Doc-rule violations fixed.** `AGENTS.md:387` requires `PLANS.md`, `pricing-config.ts`, and `plans.ts` to stay in sync on plan copy — `PLANS.md` still carried the pre-tagline Plus/Pro headings, descriptions, and upgrade-ladder framing; now mirrored. `AGENTS.md:124` still instructed that upgrade messaging position "Pro as the exam-preparation and mastery tier", the exact framing this release removed — amended, noting Board Exam Mode remains Pro-only while exam prep is no longer the tier's framing. The retry-grammar fix had shipped with **no feature-doc updates at all**, against `CLAUDE.md`'s explicit rule: `Retry Generate` corrected in `AGENTS.md:177`, `docs/features/study-pack-generation.md`, `docs/features/note-detail.md`, `docs/product/SPEC.md`, `docs/testing/study-pack.md`, and `docs/testing/mobile-ui.md` (the archive entry is left as a historical record). `docs/features/pricing.md` had never been opened despite the `/pricing` page being substantially re-messaged — positioning lines and the stale upgrade-highlight string fixed. Stale plan copy also corrected in `README.md:61` and `SPEC.md:1381,1421`.
  - **Planning-doc drift fixed, including in this release's own signoff-adjacent commit.** `05-packaging-and-terminology.md:11-12` — the ratification note added at *this release's kickoff* — still listed items 4 and 8 as open after both shipped. `ROADMAP.md:259-263` still read "Drafted, not ratified — needs an explicit owner decision before scoping" and described the rename as future work: commit `6201eaee`, titled "correct Phase 4 status," had corrected only line 9 and the Backlog Index row, leaving the Phase-Detail section contradicting both. `ROADMAP.md:274` said `fable-out/06` carried annotations on "both affected entries" when four were annotated — the same undercount `06` was corrected for, in the commit that corrected it.
  - **Four miscounts in this section corrected:** "9 test assertions across 4 suites" → **8 across 5**; "5 locations across 3 files" → **7**; "18 strings across 6 files" → **18 across 5** (the 6th contributed only a JSDoc line); and the retry bullet's "the same button label in `flashcards-page-client.tsx` and `memorization-page-client.tsx` and their shared helper prose" → **one status-card `message` prop each**, no button and no shared prose in either file. Also corrected: "nav line" in the pricing bullet was a third hero `<p>`, and that bullet's "scoped explicitly to this one surface" never accounted for `settings/page.tsx`. Verified accurate and left unchanged: `lint clean (0 errors)`, `tsc --noEmit` clean, the `library/page.tsx:1815` / `:1461` / `:836` references, `near-limit-banner.tsx:68`'s plural derivation, the two-PR `plans.ts` history, the rename PR's own "left `plans.ts` and quota copy untouched" claim, `05` §3.4's characterisation, and `fable-out/06`'s four-spot count (independently confirmed — no fifth spot exists).

### Known Limitations

Pre-existing items surfaced by the pressure test and deliberately not fixed in this release. All are **candidates for `v0.68.1`**.

- **"topic note" collides with "topic" on the bulk-generate page.** Four sentences now place both countable nouns in one clause — `bulk-generation-page-client.tsx:150,198,397,443` and `library/page.tsx:1449` — e.g. "Capped by your 3 topic notes left this cycle." rendering directly beneath a `Topics` heading, a `3 / 3` counter, and inputs labelled `Topic 1`, `Topic 2`, on a page that teaches "Each topic becomes a separate note." The retired term was at least lexically distinct. Owner decision at signoff: **keep the noun and record the cost** — it is shipped consistently across 22 sites, and a third vocabulary change inside one release is worse than the collision.
- **Two at-limit frames coexist**, both reading correctly in context but never chosen deliberately: "used all your **topic notes**" (`near-limit-banner.tsx:106`, `library/page.tsx:1449`) vs. "reached your **topic note limit**" (`note-editor-page-client.tsx:980`, `onboarding/page.tsx:1269`, `paywall-content.ts:128`). Recorded as a choice rather than left as an accident.
- **New copy promises an upgrade path the CTA resolver withholds.** `plans.ts:358-363` returns `primary: null` for PLUS + `note-generation-limit`, so `QuotaLimitBanner` renders no button — while this release's own `onboarding/page.tsx:1281` tells PLUS users "Continue with Pro if you want more room to create topic notes this month." Resolver behaviour is pre-existing; the new copy makes the dead end contradictory.
- **Both "Topic note limit reached" PRO modals are unreachable by any test.** Every note-generation-limit test uses FREE, and neither `onboarding/page.test.tsx` nor `note-editor-page-client.test.tsx` has a PRO case for this path. The two modals are also near-identical duplicates — no written rule prohibits that, but it is a live maintenance risk.
- **`app/demo/` has no test file at all**, leaving four renamed strings unpinned. `settings/page.test.tsx` pins none of the four PLUS/PRO tagline strings it renders; `/pricing` is the only surface pinning them. `PLAN_COMPARISON_ROWS`'s "Topic notes" label and `onboarding/page.tsx:1281` are likewise unpinned.
- **`noteGenerationRemainingLabel` has zero test coverage at any n** (`onboarding/page.tsx:301`, `note-editor-page-client.tsx:554`). `NearLimitBanner` with `creditLabel="topic note"` is tested only at n=2; n=1 (singular) and n=0 (component switch to `QuotaLimitBanner`) are uncovered. The bulk paste-notice path has no test at any n.
- **Two further vacuous assertions**, pre-existing and harmless: `onboarding/page.test.tsx:548,549` assert absence of "Create Again" / "Regenerate Note" buttons that exist nowhere in onboarding.
- **Companion Guidance Doctrine has four internal inconsistencies** (`AGENTS.md`): it conflicts with the Page Responsibility Rule table it claims to extend (that table assigns `Companion` one governing question, while the doctrine argues "Companion" names three structurally different things); its bullet 1 points at a table enumerating *pages*, not guidance surfaces, so a new surface has no row to look up; its "docs/copy only" header scope contradicts bullet 3's instruction to extend a resolver; and it says nothing about a new surface landing on a page with an already-grandfathered resolver. Nothing this release shipped *violates* the doctrine — the doctrine's own text is what needs reconciling, and that belongs with its still-gated Phase 1.
- **Dark-mode surface flatness on pricing cards.** `--surface-alt` in `.dark` is the same hue as `--background` at 40% alpha, so `Card`'s `bg-surface-alt` composites to the background colour and every plan card reads only via its border; the Free card's dashed "Paid upgrades" box (`bg-background/70`) is likewise borderless-flat. Same root-cause family as the `v0.66.2` `bg-card` token bug (`--card: var(--background)` makes `bg-card` a no-op in both themes). Possibly intentional — borders carry the structure — but unverified and untouched.
- **`paywall-content.ts`'s `PRIMARY_CTA_LABEL` / `SECONDARY_CTA_LABEL` are dead code**: written at 13 sites in that file and read nowhere under `app|components|lib|src`. Untouched by this release, but a live trap — a future surface reading `presentation.primaryCtaLabel` would silently bypass `getUpgradeCtas`.
- **Two planning docs still describe `Generate Note` as a shipped, sanctioned feature** — `note-preview-vs-summary-out/01-card-content-strategy.md` (8 refs) and its session plan (5 refs). Not purely historical: `ROADMAP.md:1039` cites that document's "Explicit rejections §3–4" as still-binding anti-drift. `docs/features/public-library.md:443` names "the shipped `Generate Note` feature" twice, once inside a standing "do not build" rule.
- **`docs/gpt-contexts/GPT_CONTEXT.md` is stamped `v0.67.0`** and retains pre-rename copy. Correct by design — `CLAUDE.md` describes it as a version-stamped snapshot — but it is a source a future session may read.

## v0.67.1 - Explore Convergence Follow-ups

**Status: Released**

Theme: fix `v0.67.1`-candidate findings logged as Known Limitations at `v0.67.0` signoff — Explore's hardcoded "Review Sets" tab label colliding with `BOARD_EXAM`'s Collections nav label, Exam Hub's official-set card hardcoding `adoptedCollection={null}`, the zero-note Dashboard's two stacked Explore CTAs, and (added mid-release, 2026-07-31, once noticed the first pass had scoped in only 3 of the 4 items actually tagged `Candidate for v0.67.1` in `v0.67.0`'s Known Limitations) `PublishedPlansPageClient`'s now-unreachable `?ref=` contextual back-link. A 5th, unrelated item — a dark-mode contrast bug on Explore's own segmented control, not a `v0.67.0` Known Limitation and not caught by any of this release's own tests — was found from a live screenshot and folded in the same day. Two further `v0.67.0` Known Limitations that were *not* originally tagged `Candidate for v0.67.1` (they weren't scoped as quick patches) were folded in on explicit owner review of the remaining list: `server-public-study-plans.ts`'s type-cast crash risk and `DashboardCommunityNotesSection`'s unslugified Explore filter link. Together these seven items meant the original three-candidate scoping under-counted its own release by more than half — worth naming plainly: the `v0.67.0` pressure test's own "Candidate for `v0.67.1`" tagging was itself incomplete (missed the back-link item), and neither of the two untagged pre-existing bugs were surfaced until an explicit owner review of the full Known Limitations list, not by any automated process. A pre-signoff full pressure test (four parallel review agents, gated by this release crossing 4+ merged PRs and `explore-page-client.tsx` being touched by two separate PRs) found and fixed two further gaps before signoff — see Shipped below.

### Planned Scope

- **Explore tab label collision (frontend only).** `getCollectionLabels()` already exists for exactly this profile-branching problem — route Explore's segmented-control label through it (or otherwise disambiguate) instead of the hardcoded "Review Sets" string, so `BOARD_EXAM` profiles no longer see the same label point at two different destinations (personal Collections from nav, public Official Review Set catalog from inside Explore).
- **Exam Hub official-set card `adoptedCollection` (frontend only).** Resolve the real `adoptedCollection` value the same way the `/collections/published` sibling caller does, instead of hardcoding `null`, so an already-adopted signed-in visitor sees "Continue" instead of "Start" and doesn't re-trigger the (idempotent) adopt call.
- **Zero-note Dashboard duplicate Explore CTAs (frontend only).** `DashboardEmpty`'s "start from a ready-made plan instead" and `DashboardStudyPlanSection`'s pointer card both link to the identical `/explore?source=dashboard` and now stack directly on top of each other. Product call made: keep `DashboardEmpty`'s inline link (its copy fits first-run), suppress the pointer card there instead — see Shipped below.
- **`PublishedPlansPageClient`'s unreachable `?ref=` back-link (frontend only).** The original `v0.67.0` finding assumed the whole `?ref=` allowlist mechanism was dead and needed a "restore a producer, or retire the mechanism" design call. Investigation found it wasn't fully dead: `PublicLibraryPageClient`'s "Browse official plans →" bridge still navigates to `/collections/published` today, it just never attached `?ref=`. Restore that one producer on the standalone `/public/library` page. The embedded (Explore Notes tab) case needs a different fix, not a new `ref=` value: per `AGENTS.md`'s Page Responsibility Rule ("Explore is the single owner of content discovery... other pages may point at Explore; they may not do Explore's job"), an Explore-embedded visitor should switch to Explore's own Review Sets tab rather than be sent out to the standalone route at all.
- **Explore segmented-control dark-mode contrast (frontend only).** The selected tab's `bg-background` is visually indistinguishable from the tablist container's `bg-surface-alt` in dark mode — both resolve to the same near-black (`--surface-alt` is a translucent version of the same `--background` color in `.dark`), so the selected state disappears. Same root-cause class as the `v0.66.2` `bg-card` token bug. Fix by mimicking the Settings page's billing-cycle toggle, which already solves this with an explicit `bg-blue-600 ... dark:bg-blue-500` pill rather than a semantic surface token.
- **`server-public-study-plans.ts` type-cast crash risk (frontend only).** A malformed-but-200 backend response (valid JSON, but not an array) previously parsed without error, then threw uncaught downstream in the caller's `result.value.forEach(...)` — a crash risk specific to the anonymous, SEO-relevant Exam Hub page's server render. Guard with `Array.isArray(parsed) ? parsed : []` at the parse boundary, mirroring the sibling `server-public-notes.ts`'s defensive `payload?.items ?? []` access pattern rather than trusting the `as` cast.
- **`DashboardCommunityNotesSection`'s unslugified Explore filter link (frontend only).** Its `buildExploreUrl({ filters: { courseProgram } })` call passed the raw, unslugified course/program value — every other caller of `buildPublicLibraryUrl`/`buildExploreUrl` with a `courseProgram` filter wraps it in `slugifyPublicLibraryFilterValue()` first. Wrap it here too, so the arriving filter chip displays on Explore's Notes tab and a no-op Filters-modal re-submit no longer silently drops it. Pre-existing bug (traced via `git blame` to a commit predating `v0.67.0`), not introduced by this release, but this release's own Dashboard-pointer work touches the exact same link.

Anti-drift: the first five are targeted fixes to `v0.67.0`-shipped code (the segmented control's `bg-background` selected-state styling shipped as part of `v0.67.0`'s original `/explore` page, unchanged by this release's own tab-label fix above); the last two are pre-existing bugs predating `v0.67.0` entirely, each an isolated 1-2 file fix with a clear root cause. No new surfaces, no scope beyond the seven logged findings.

### Shipped

- **Explore tab label collision (frontend only).** `explore-page-client.tsx`'s Review Sets tab now reads `Official ${labels.plural}` (resolved via `getCollectionLabels(authUser?.profileType)`), not the bare profile-aware label — routing through bare `labels.plural` alone would have kept the collision, since `BOARD_EXAM`'s Collections nav label is itself literally "Review Sets." The `Official` prefix matches the phrasing `/collections/published` already uses inside that same panel ("Browse All Official {plural}"). The tab's internal `id` (`"review-sets"`), its query param, and its `EXPLORE_TAB_SWITCHED` analytics value are untouched — label only. The longer prefixed labels (`Official Lesson Plans`, `Official Study Plans`) run close to the available width in the two-tab `grid-cols-2` segmented control at narrow phone viewports, so the tab button text dropped to `text-xs sm:text-sm` (previously always `text-sm`) to keep it on one line; `grid-cols-2` would have kept the layout intact either way (equal-width cells, no overflow), so this is a headroom improvement, not a fix for broken layout. Test coverage added for the `BOARD_EXAM` case specifically.
- **Exam Hub official-set card `adoptedCollection` (frontend only).** `ExamHubOfficialReviewSets` now client-side fetches the signed-in visitor's own collections and resolves `adoptedCollection` by matching `sourcePlanId`, the same resolution `/collections/published` uses, instead of hardcoding `null`. Fails open to no adopted collection on a lookup error, consistent with this section's existing anonymous-page contract. Test coverage added, including the fail-open path. Two tradeoffs accepted for this patch, both logged under Known Limitations below rather than left mid-paragraph here. **Pre-signoff pressure-test fix:** the raw fetch's response was cast with `as NoteCollectionSummary[]` without validating it was actually an array — the exact bug class `server-public-study-plans.ts`'s fix (below) closed elsewhere in this same release, missed here because it's a different file with an independently-written fetch. Now guarded with the same `Array.isArray()` check; regression test added for the malformed-but-200 case.
- **Zero-note Dashboard duplicate Explore CTAs (frontend only).** `DashboardStudyPlanSection` gains `suppressPointerWhenNoPrimary?: boolean`; when set, its no-primary `ExplorePointerCard` branch renders nothing instead of a second CTA. Passed `true` only from the STUDENT/PROFESSIONAL zero-note Dashboard call site (`totalNoteCount === 0`), where `DashboardEmpty`'s own inline "Or start from a ready-made {plan} instead" link already covers the same destination — chosen over deleting `DashboardEmpty`'s link because its copy is the one that actually fits a user who has never had a plan (the pointer card's heading, "Find your next {plan}," reads oddly for a true first-run visitor). The valid-primary continue-card branch is unaffected either way. Board Exam's zero-note branch never rendered `DashboardEmpty` and was never duplicated, so its call site is untouched. Test coverage added at both the component level (suppression behavior, primary-continue unaffected) and the page level (prop wiring, scoped correctly to the STUDENT/PROFESSIONAL branch only).
- **`PublishedPlansPageClient`'s unreachable `?ref=` back-link (frontend only).** `PublicLibraryPageClient`'s "Browse official plans →" bridge now attaches `?ref=/public/library` on the standalone `/public/library` page, instead of a bare href with no `ref` at all — this link never carried `ref=` since its introduction (`f5ac692b`), so it's a gap being closed, not a v0.67.0 regression. This producer only changes behavior for a **signed-in** visitor on standalone `/public/library`: the no-ref fallback is `canAdopt ? "/dashboard" : "/public/library"`, so an anonymous visitor already got "Public Library" for free and this is a no-op for them; a signed-in visitor previously got the generic "Dashboard" fallback and now correctly returns to "Public Library," where they actually came from. When rendered embedded (Explore's Notes tab), the same bridge now links to a module-level `EXPLORE_REVIEW_SETS_PATH` (`buildExploreUrl({ tab: "review-sets" })`, i.e. `/explore`) instead of leaving Explore for the standalone route at all — required by `AGENTS.md`'s Page Responsibility Rule doctrine (an initial version of this fix routed the embedded case through `/collections/published?ref=/explore` with a new allowlist case; caught before commit and corrected to stay inside Explore instead, since `PublishedPlansPageClient` sources its course/program from `getMe()` in both places, so nothing is lost by switching tabs rather than navigating away). `/dashboard` and `/collections` remain allowlisted with no live producer today — verified via `git log -S`, both had real producers (`ref=/dashboard`, `ref=/collections`) that the "Explore Owns Discovery" commits (`c4ae5f5a`, `455ed669`) intentionally removed when those pointers were rerouted through Explore; that orphaning was the deliberate, doctrine-driven outcome of that release, not an oversight, so left as-is here. `published-plans-page-client.test.tsx`'s 4 existing ref-allowlist cases are unchanged (no new case added, since the embedded fix no longer produces a new `ref=` value). Test coverage added for the standalone `?ref=/public/library` producer and for the embedded case staying inside Explore.

- **Explore segmented-control dark-mode contrast (frontend only).** Found from a live dark-mode screenshot, not the pressure test — the selected tab was effectively invisible in `.dark`. `explore-page-client.tsx`'s selected-tab class changed from `bg-background text-foreground shadow-sm` to `bg-blue-600 text-white shadow-sm dark:bg-blue-500 dark:text-slate-950`, matching the exact pattern the Settings page's billing-cycle toggle (`app/settings/page.tsx`) already uses for the same problem — an explicit color instead of a semantic surface token that collapses against its own container in dark mode. Applies in both themes (a blue pill, not a theme-conditional swap), consistent with the Settings precedent. Regression test added asserting the selected tab's class contains `bg-blue-600` and not `bg-background`. **Pre-signoff pressure-test fix:** the pre-existing `focus-visible:ring-blue-600` ring now matches the new `bg-blue-600` fill color exactly, so tabbing to the already-selected tab produced a much weaker focus indicator than the mouse-selected state the owner screenshot-verified. Added `focus-visible:ring-offset-2` (matching `components/ui/button.tsx`'s existing focus-ring pattern) to restore separation; regression test extended to assert the offset class.
- **`server-public-study-plans.ts` type-cast crash risk (frontend only).** `getServerPublicStudyPlansByCourseProgram` now validates the parsed response with `Array.isArray()` before returning it, degrading to `[]` instead of letting a malformed-but-200 response propagate an uncaught `TypeError` out of the caller's `.forEach`. Regression test added: a mocked `{ error: "..." }` 200 response between two otherwise-valid lookups now resolves with just the valid result instead of rejecting the whole `Promise`.
- **`DashboardCommunityNotesSection`'s unslugified Explore filter link (frontend only).** Its Explore link now slugifies `courseProgram` via `slugifyPublicLibraryFilterValue()` before building the URL, matching every other `buildPublicLibraryUrl`/`buildExploreUrl` caller. The existing test asserting the raw unslugified value in the URL (`?courseProgram=Medical – Surgical Nursing`) was itself encoding the bug as expected behavior; corrected to assert the slugified value (`medical-surgical-nursing`).

Not browser-verified by this session: local backend/Postgres were not running, so most fixes are verified via `tsc --noEmit`, lint, and the full Jest suite (162 suites / 1638 tests) with test coverage asserting the exact rendered text/attributes/hrefs, not a live render. The dark-mode contrast fix was owner-confirmed in a real browser, both themes, on the actual running app — screenshots showed the selected tab as a clear blue pill in both `Notes` (dark and light) and `Official Study Plans` (unselected, correctly dim) states, matching the Settings billing-toggle reference exactly.

**Pre-signoff full pressure test (2026-07-31).** This release crossed this project's own full-pressure-test gate — 5 merged PRs by this point, and `explore-page-client.tsx` touched by two separate PRs (the tab-label fix and the dark-mode contrast fix) — so four parallel review agents inventoried every file touched across the release rather than a single `advisor()` pass, plus one further agent fact-checking RELEASES.md's own claims (git history, test counts, doc accuracy) against the real repo, since this project has twice previously shipped a confidently-worded but false scoping claim. Findings: two real, fixed before signoff (both above); one real doc-drift finding in `ROADMAP.md`'s Current Release Baseline (fixed as part of this signoff, see `v0.67.1`'s own kickoff-vs-actual-scope note above); two minor test-coverage gaps logged below rather than fixed, since they're not functional gaps. Every other claim this session had made about this release — the `git log -S` back-link history, the `f5ac692b` no-`ref=` claim, the doc updates, the diff hygiene, the Known Limitations completeness, the raw-fetch redirect-avoidance claim — was independently verified true against the real code and history, not just re-asserted.

### Known Limitations

- **Exam Hub official-set card's `adoptedCollection` resolution can briefly show "Start" before settling to "Continue."** The card's `plans` prop is server-rendered and available immediately; the client-side collections fetch that resolves `adoptedCollection` lands afterward. An already-adopted authenticated visitor can see the stale "Start" label for that window and click it — harmless (the backend adopt path is idempotent), but not gated behind a loading state this patch.
- **`EXPLORE_OFFICIAL_SET_ADOPT_CLICKED` now also fails to fire for already-adopted Exam Hub viewers who click "Continue."** Consistent with `/collections/published`'s existing behavior (both surfaces now share the same skewed-conversion-rate limitation logged at `v0.67.0` signoff), not a new gap introduced here — but the affected surface area grew, since Exam Hub's hardcoded `adoptedCollection={null}` had been masking it there specifically.
- **No single test exercises the `BOARD_EXAM` tab label and the dark-mode `bg-blue-600` selected class together.** Each is tested individually (different PRs, different tests); combined-render coverage would be a nicety, not a functional gap — found by the pre-signoff pressure test, not fixed.
- **No page-level test asserts a non-zero-note STUDENT/PROFESSIONAL Dashboard does NOT receive `suppressPointerWhenNoPrimary: true`.** Only the zero-note case is asserted true, and the BOARD_EXAM case is asserted not-true; the component-level tests already prove `usingPrimary` correctly short-circuits suppression regardless, so this is coverage, not correctness — found by the pre-signoff pressure test, not fixed.

## v0.67.0 - Explore Convergence

**Status: Released**

Theme: give signed-in users one nav-level surface ("Explore") to discover both curated Official Review Sets and the public notes library, and let the Exam Hub point straight into an official-set adoption path when one exists for that hub's course/program — closing the "post-signup in-app discovery" gap this release's own gate was written to test, independent of the still-open pre-signup acquisition-channel question.

**Gate override, ratified by the owner 2026-07-30.** This release's stated gate — "proceed only if the Diagnostic Read indicates a discovery problem" — is unmet; Round 2 of the read isn't due until after 2026-08-06. The owner explicitly chose to override and kick off now rather than wait, consistent with this project's established precedent for owner-ratified gate overrides (Knowledge Impact, `v0.62.0`; Challenge Quiz Quota Increase, `v0.61.0` — both shipped ahead of their own data gates on explicit owner reasoning, not silently). Recorded as history, not smoothed over: see `docs/product/ROADMAP.md`'s "Phase 2 — IA / Explore convergence" section and Current Release Baseline for the full ratification note. On record, not a blocker: the 2026-07-24 signup surge that renewed interest in this gate is Facebook/LET-direct-landing traffic — pre-signup acquisition-channel discovery, a mechanism distinct from the post-signup in-app discovery this release actually addresses (`ROADMAP.md` line 213) — if that segment's retention doesn't move regardless of this release, Explore didn't touch the real mechanism, and that's a fact for the eventual Diagnostic Read re-read to surface, not a reason to hold back given the override.

**Checkpoint committed at kickoff, 2026-07-30.** Per this project's `[CHECKPOINT — due YYYY-MM-DD]` discipline (see `ROADMAP.md`'s Backlog Index gate-types note): ship a new `AnalyticsEventType` firing on Explore nav-item engagement (segmented-control tab switch, and adopt/preview actions taken from within Explore or the Exam Hub's new official-set path) in this same release, then re-check 30-45 days after ship (**due 2026-09-13**) whether Explore-driven adopt/preview activity meaningfully exceeds what the old direct `/collections/published` and `/public/library` nav entries drove pre-launch. Kill criterion: if Explore-attributed engagement is flat-to-negligible against the pre-launch baseline, treat that as evidence the discovery gap was not the retention lever, not a reason to keep iterating on this surface without new evidence.

### Planned Scope

- **Nav restructure (frontend only).** `frontend/components/app-shell.tsx` (`MAIN_NAV`) and `frontend/components/mobile-bottom-tab-bar.tsx`: replace the "Public Library" nav item with a new "Explore" item; desktop reorders to Dashboard / Collections (existing dynamic `getCollectionLabels().navLabel`, profile-branched — not a new literal "My Reviews" string, which does not exist anywhere in code today) / Library / Explore / Progress. Mobile's bottom tab bar gets the same "Public Library" → "Explore" swap (owner-confirmed same mapping as desktop), keeping its existing 4-tab shape and backend-persisted show/hide preference (`user.mobileTabBarEnabled`) untouched.
- **New `/explore` page (frontend only).** Segmented control compositing the existing `/collections/published` (Official Review Set catalog) and `/public/library` (public notes library) content, plus a pointer card into the Exam Hub index. `/collections/published` and `/public/library` remain live as real, separately-addressable routes (deep links, SEO) — `/explore` is a nav-level composite, not a new canonical content URL.
- **`PlanPicker` extraction (frontend only).** Currently a private, unexported function component inside `frontend/app/progress/progress-report-client.tsx:322`, used only at line 824 in the same file. Extract into a shared component (preserving its existing `?collectionId=` behavior unchanged) so Explore's adopt flow can reuse it rather than rebuilding adopt UI from scratch.
- **`/exam/[slug]` additive official-set check (frontend + backend-adjacent).** `frontend/app/exam/[slug]/page.tsx` currently resolves public notes only via `getServerPublicNotesByCoursePrograms`. Add: resolve the hub's `coursePrograms` array against published Official Review Sets, looping the backend's existing `courseProgram`-exact-match-only `NoteCollectionController.listPublic`/`NoteCollectionService.listPublic` (confirmed: no subject-level or fuzzy matching exists — the additive check can only honestly claim courseProgram-exact matching, matching the same false-precision constraint already documented on the `v0.64.0` Ask-Companion-scoping Known Limitation). A match adds a preview+adopt path reusing the extracted `PlanPicker`.
- **Explore-engagement `AnalyticsEventType`.** New event(s) covering segmented-control tab switches and adopt/preview actions taken from Explore or the Exam Hub's new official-set path — the checkpoint above depends on this shipping in the same release, not being added later.

Anti-drift: Library stays untouched and structurally separate from Collections — Explore composites, it does not merge, the two catalogs. Adopt-sets-Primary-default is unchanged; this release does not resolve the open Primary-Review-Set-vs-Study/Exam-Focus philosophy question (flagged, not fixed, per the original source doc). No literal "My Reviews" string is introduced anywhere — the Collections nav item keeps its existing profile-dependent dynamic label. Progress's promotion to first-class nav (no `← Dashboard` back link) already shipped in `v0.59.0` and is not re-touched here.

### Shipped

Implemented by Codex from a Long-mode prompt (run once, not retained in the repo), audited against the real diff by an independent fresh-context Opus review (not a summary trust) before signoff — see Known Limitations below for what that audit found and deferred.

- **"Explore Owns Discovery" scope addition — Collections workspace and shared URL contract (2026-07-30).** Added `frontend/lib/explore-url.ts` as the canonical source for Explore paths, tabs, pointer-source metadata, and Public Library filter composition. Explore page views now carry memoized `source` metadata without re-firing on unrelated renders. `/collections` is a pure owned workspace: its embedded `DashboardStudyPlanSection` discovery catalog and unused course/program state are removed, while its profile-aware header CTA points to `/explore?source=collections`. `AGENTS.md`, SPEC, Collections docs, and the roadmap now lock Explore as the single authenticated discovery owner while preserving `/collections/published` and `/public/library` as canonical standalone routes.
- **"Explore Owns Discovery" scope addition — Dashboard pointers (2026-07-30).** `DashboardStudyPlanSection` now defaults to its unchanged full onboarding presentation but both persistent Dashboard call sites use a no-catalog `"pointer"` presentation: primary lookup still gates rendering, valid-primary continue mode is unchanged, and no/stale primary renders one dashed `/explore?source=dashboard` pointer without calling `listPublicStudyPlans`. Removed the dead `viewAllHref`/`browseWhenEmpty`/match-count paths. The bounded four-note community teaser now sends its see-all link to Explore's Notes tab with course/program and source preserved, and `DashboardEmpty` sends its ready-made-set link through Explore. No backend or onboarding changes. This intentionally supersedes the v0.39.1 cold-start "no curated {plural} for {program} yet... browse every official set" card on the two Dashboard call sites — that specific empty-state copy is retired, not silently dropped, because Explore's pointer now owns that no-match state for Dashboard; onboarding's own call site is untouched and keeps the original card via `"full"` presentation.
- **Authenticated Explore navigation.** Desktop navigation now reads Dashboard / profile-aware Collections label / Library / Explore / Progress, and the optional four-item mobile tab bar replaces Public Library with Explore while preserving its existing preference gate. The separate public marketing navbar is unchanged.
- **Composite `/explore` page.** Explore presents Review Sets and Notes behind an accessible URL-backed segmented control, reusing the existing `/collections/published` catalog and `/public/library` data/rendering with independently scoped loading, error, and retry behavior. The original routes remain live and behaviorally unchanged, and Explore includes a pointer to the Exam Hub index.
- **Shared plan picker and Exam Hub enrichment.** The Progress plan picker is now a shared component with its existing `/progress?collectionId=` behavior intact (regression-verified: same default props as the deleted inline version). Exam Hubs perform one best-effort published-set lookup per configured `courseProgram`, dedupe on collection id (a collection matching 2+ of a hub's `coursePrograms` — e.g. PNLE's `Nursing` and `Medical – Surgical Nursing` — appears once, not twice), and show an anonymous-safe preview/adopt section when matches exist; the lookup fails open (try/catch on both `fetch` and `.json()`, wrapped in `Promise.allSettled`) so a lookup failure never blanks the anonymous, SEO-relevant hub page. Signed-out adoption preserves exam intent via the existing `notelib-exam-intent` cookie and routes through `/auth`.
- **Discovery analytics, including a checkpoint-measurability fix found at audit.** Explore tab switches plus Official Review Set preview/adopt clicks from Explore and Exam Hub emit non-blocking events declared in both frontend and backend analytics event contracts (verified 5-for-5 parity, all five confirmed actually firing at their call sites). The audit also found the delivered scope had **no page-view event for `/explore` itself** — `EXPLORE_TAB_SWITCHED` only fires on an actual tab change, and default-tab browsing emitted nothing — which would have made this release's own `[CHECKPOINT — due 2026-09-13]` unmeasurable. Added `EXPLORE_VIEWED` (fires once on mount, no metadata, mirroring `PUBLISHED_PLANS_VIEWED`'s existing pattern) to close that gap before signoff.
- **Doc-accuracy corrections found at audit.** `docs/features/exam-hub.md` no longer claims the anonymous `redirect` query param returns a brand-new signup to the hub post-auth — `resolvePostLoginDestination` actually sends new signups through `/verify-email`/`/onboarding` first, dropping `redirect`; only the `notelib-exam-intent` cookie carries context on that path in practice. `docs/features/navigation.md`'s back-link table now reflects that a Public Note Details back link reads "Explore" (not "Public Library") when the visitor arrived via `/explore`'s Notes tab.
- **Minor cleanup found at audit.** Removed the now-fully-unused `publicLibrary` `ActionIconName` (redundant with the existing `public` icon, same glyph) per the original prompt's cleanup instruction; `server-public-study-plans.ts` now declares `API_BASE_URL` locally instead of importing it as a value from the client-oriented `lib/api.ts`, matching its sibling server-fetch files; a stale comment in `public-library-url.ts` referencing a since-removed mobile-tab-bar reader was corrected.
- **Pre-signoff pressure test (2026-07-31) and its one pre-signoff fix.** Explore's release-wide surface area (nav, `/explore`, Collections, Dashboard, Exam Hub, and both frontend/backend analytics contracts, spanning 3 PRs with several files touched by more than one) cleared this project's own full-pressure-test bar rather than a single `advisor()` pass. Four parallel review agents inventoried every touched file; findings and dispositions below. One gap threatened this release's own checkpoint measurability and was fixed before signoff, the same handling this release already gave the missing `EXPLORE_VIEWED` event above: `EXPLORE_OFFICIAL_SET_PREVIEWED`/`ADOPT_CLICKED` never carried the dashboard/collections pointer-origin metadata that `EXPLORE_VIEWED` already had, because `explore-page-client.tsx` never threaded a `discoveryMetadata` prop down through `PublishedPlansPageClient` to `PublicStudyPlanCard` — silently breaking the pointer-vs-direct segmentation the `[CHECKPOINT — due 2026-09-13]` needs for its adopt/preview comparison. Fixed via PR #952 (`fix/explore-pointer-source-attribution`), keyed as `pointerSource` specifically to avoid colliding with the card's own `{ ...discoveryMetadata, source: discoverySource }` merge, which would have silently overwritten a same-named key. Test coverage added at all three touched layers, since missing coverage is why the original gap wasn't caught by either PR's own audit.

### Known Limitations

- **Dashboard's valid-primary continue mode still duplicates `DashboardPrimaryCollectionHero`.** Users with a Primary Review Set see the same owned set in the condensed hero and again lower on the page through `DashboardStudyPlanSection`. Deferred per explicit owner decision; this pointer pass changes only no-primary discovery presentation.
- **Dashboard's two no-course/program guidance treatments remain inconsistent.** `DashboardStudyPlanSection` pointer mode shows only its Explore pointer, while `DashboardCommunityNotesSection` still renders the larger existing course/program card plus modal for the same learner state. The community section remains a compliant bounded teaser and was structurally out of scope, so its nudge was not redesigned here.
- **`/explore` mounts and fetches both tabs' data on every load, not just the active one.** The inactive panel is only `hidden`, so a page view triggers the full data-fetch chain for both Review Sets and Notes even though roughly half of it is invisible. The original prompt required error-state *isolation* between tabs (delivered — each has its own retry state) but didn't ask for lazy mounting, so this is a real but out-of-scope performance gap, not a spec violation. Not fixed this release; a follow-up would gate each panel's fetch behind its own `activeTab === id` check instead of `hidden`.
- **A pre-existing sessionStorage-based back-link bug widens its blast radius.** `PublicLibraryBackLink` reads a stored return URL that can go stale if a user reaches a public note through a non-saving path (a shared link, organic search) after previously saving a different filtered view — the label can lie about where "back" actually goes. This bug predates this release; Explore's addition means the stale label can now read "Explore" for a page the user never visited, not just a stale "Public Library" filter. Not fixed this release — the underlying sessionStorage mechanism is unrelated to Explore's own scope.
- **`PublishedPlansPageClient`'s `?ref=`-based contextual back-link is now unreachable from real navigation.** Both pointer-adding PRs this release (Collections workspace, Dashboard pointers) removed their own `?ref=` producer when they switched to Explore-routed links instead; grepping the app confirms zero remaining producers of `ref=` into `/collections/published` anywhere. Every real visitor to that page now gets the generic default back-link instead of a contextual one — and more subtly, `published-plans-page-client.test.tsx`'s 4 dedicated ref-allowlist tests stay green while exercising a code path that can no longer fire from real navigation, so passing tests alone would misrepresent this as covered. Not fixed this release — needs a design call (restore a producer, or retire the dead code and its tests), not a mechanical patch. Distinct from the pre-existing sessionStorage back-link bug above — different component, different mechanism. **Fixed in `v0.67.1`:** the "zero remaining producers" claim above turned out to be about the `?ref=` param specifically, not navigation to the page — `PublicLibraryPageClient`'s "Browse official plans →" link still navigated there, it just never attached `?ref=`. Restored `ref=/public/library` on the standalone page; the embedded (Explore) case was fixed by staying inside Explore entirely instead, per `AGENTS.md`'s Page Responsibility Rule — see `v0.67.1`'s own Shipped section.
- **Explore's segmented-control tab label hardcodes "Review Sets"** instead of routing through `getCollectionLabels()`. For `BOARD_EXAM` profiles, whose Collections nav label is also "Review Sets," the same string now points at two different destinations — personal Collections from nav, the public Official Review Set catalog from inside Explore. An anti-drift gap this release's own per-PR audits didn't catch, since the collision is with an earlier-shipped feature (`getCollectionLabels`), not with anything else in this release. Not fixed this release. **Fixed in `v0.67.1`:** the tab label now reads `Official ${labels.plural}` instead of the bare profile-aware label — see `v0.67.1`'s own Shipped section.
- **Exam Hub's official-set card hardcodes `adoptedCollection={null}`**, unlike its sibling caller in `/collections/published`, which resolves the real value. A signed-in visitor who already adopted that set sees "Start" instead of "Continue" and re-triggers the adopt call. The backend adopt path is idempotent (unique index plus an existing-adoption short-circuit), so no duplicate collection results — a mislabeled-CTA and redundant-round-trip bug, not a data-integrity one. No existing test exercises an authenticated, already-adopted Exam Hub visitor, which is why this wasn't caught earlier. Not fixed this release. **Fixed in `v0.67.1`:** `adoptedCollection` is now resolved from the visitor's real collections instead of hardcoded `null` — see `v0.67.1`'s own Shipped section.
- **The zero-note Dashboard shows two near-duplicate Explore pointer CTAs stacked directly on top of each other** — `DashboardEmpty`'s "start from a ready-made plan instead" and `DashboardStudyPlanSection`'s new pointer card both link to the identical `/explore?source=dashboard`. Before this release's Dashboard-pointer change, the second card showed substantive matched-plan/cold-start content, so the two weren't near-duplicates; collapsing it to a bare pointer created the stacking. Untested — the zero-note Dashboard test mocks `DashboardStudyPlanSection` out entirely. Needs a product call on which CTA wins, not a mechanical fix. Not fixed this release. **Fixed in `v0.67.1`:** `DashboardStudyPlanSection`'s pointer card is now suppressed on the zero-note Dashboard, leaving `DashboardEmpty`'s inline link as the sole CTA — see `v0.67.1`'s own Shipped section.
- **A fast double-click on an inactive Explore tab can fire `EXPLORE_TAB_SWITCHED` twice for one logical switch.** `selectTab` guards only against the currently-committed URL tab, not an in-flight navigation, so two rapid clicks before the `router.replace`-triggered re-render lands can both pass the stale-state check. The window is roughly 1-2 frames for a query-only Next.js navigation — well under real double-click timing — so this is unlikely to reproduce from an actual user, but it is a real analytics-quality gap. Not fixed this release.
- **`EXPLORE_OFFICIAL_SET_PREVIEWED` fires on every preview open, including re-opens, while `EXPLORE_OFFICIAL_SET_ADOPT_CLICKED` only fires when the viewer has not already adopted the set** — a "Continue this plan" click on an already-adopted set emits nothing. Any preview-to-adopt conversion rate computed from these two events will read skewed low relative to actual adoption activity. Not fixed this release. **Extended by a `v0.67.1` fix:** Exam Hub's official-set card previously hardcoded `adoptedCollection={null}`, so this gap was masked there — every Exam Hub adopt click fired regardless of prior adoption. `v0.67.1` fixed the hardcoding (see its own Shipped section), which means Exam Hub now exhibits this same pre-existing skew instead of masking it. Not a new bug, but the affected surface area grew.
- **`server-public-study-plans.ts` asserts its backend response shape via a type cast instead of validating it at runtime.** A malformed but 200-status response would throw uncaught inside the function instead of degrading gracefully the way its sibling public-notes fetch function already does — a crash risk specific to the anonymous, SEO-relevant Exam Hub page. Not fixed this release. **Fixed in `v0.67.1`:** guarded with `Array.isArray()` at the parse boundary — see `v0.67.1`'s own Shipped section.
- **Explore's embedded Notes tab drops the "Share this list" affordance** that the standalone `/public/library` page offers, since `PageHeader`'s `actions` slot is skipped in embedded mode and Explore adds no replacement. A capability loss, not a bug; not fixed this release.
- **Pre-existing, not introduced by this release, logged for visibility since this release's own Dashboard-pointer work touches the same link:** `DashboardCommunityNotesSection` builds its Explore link's course/program filter from the raw, unslugified value, unlike every other caller, which slugifies first. The arriving filter chip never displays, and re-submitting the Filters modal without changing anything silently drops the working filter. Traced via `git blame` to a commit predating this release; carried over unfixed by this release's link retargeting. **Fixed in `v0.67.1`:** now wraps the value in `slugifyPublicLibraryFilterValue()` before building the URL — see `v0.67.1`'s own Shipped section.

## v0.66.2 - Card Surface Token Fix

**Status: Released**

Theme: fix `bg-card`, a Tailwind utility with 23 literal usages across 9 files (22 rendered elements) that has silently resolved to no output since it was first used, because no `--color-card` token was ever registered in `globals.css`.

### Planned Scope

- **Register `--color-card` (frontend only, `frontend/app/globals.css`).** Alias `--card` to `--background` in both `:root`/`.theme-light` and `.theme-dark`/`.dark`, and register `--color-card: var(--card)` in the `@theme inline` block, so `bg-card` finally compiles. `--background` was chosen over `--surface-alt` because standalone nested panels — `ScoreReveal` on the Challenge Quiz result screen and `public-flashcards-preview.tsx` — sit inside a parent `Card` (`bg-surface-alt`); aliasing `--card` to `--surface-alt` would flatten them into a same-tone stack with no visible separation, while `--background` gives them real contrast against their container. (Two other call sites, `challenge-quiz/page.tsx:2090` and `long-exam/page.tsx:1090`, apply `bg-card` directly on a `Card` component alongside `Card`'s own default `bg-surface-alt` — an independent audit found this does **not** actually override the background: `cn()` is a plain string join with no `tailwind-merge`, and the compiled stylesheet places `.bg-card` before `.bg-surface-alt`, so equal-specificity cascade order means `bg-surface-alt` wins regardless of which token `--card` aliases to. Confirmed directly against the live compiled CSS. This doesn't change which token is correct — the standalone-panel evidence above still holds — but the original rationale citing these two sites as a "preserved override" was wrong and has been removed.)
- **Not a uniform no-op-to-no-op change.** Most of the 23 usages are visually unchanged (previously transparent/border-only, now filled with `--background`, which reads the same in an un-nested context). Two are genuine, verified-good changes: `quiz-generation-overlay.tsx`'s modal panel goes from a translucent blur-bleed-through card to a solid, legible dialog panel (checked in both themes via a real render — clean in both), and nested panels (`ScoreReveal`, `public-flashcards-preview`) gain real separation from their container instead of blending into it.

Anti-drift: CSS token registration only — no component logic, className, or markup changes; all 23 call sites pick up the fix automatically with no per-site edits.

### Shipped

- **Registered `--color-card` in `frontend/app/globals.css`, aliased to `--background`.** `bg-card` now compiles across all 23 usages (22 rendered elements) with no per-site changes. See Planned Scope above for the corrected token-choice rationale and the two genuinely-changed cases (verified in both themes).
- **Backlog Index corrected:** the `bg-card` no-op row now reflects the real count (23 usages across 9 files, 22 elements) and the corrected reasoning.
- **Independent audit caught a factual error before signoff:** the original scoping claimed two `Card`+`bg-card` call sites were "deliberately overriding" `Card`'s default background, and that this fix "preserved" that override. A fresh audit found the override never actually applied (`cn()` has no `tailwind-merge`, and compiled-CSS order decides equal-specificity ties, not JSX class order) — confirmed directly against the live stylesheet. The token choice itself was unaffected (it rests on separate, valid nested-panel evidence), but the false claim was removed rather than left standing.

## v0.66.1 - Goal Detail Due-Concept Signal

**Status: Released**

Theme: give a Goal's child Subject-plan cards a lightweight visual cue for concepts due for spaced-repetition review, and formalize an existing undocumented anti-drift exception found while scoping it.

### Planned Scope

- **Doc fix: formalize the Goal-detail readiness exception (docs only).** `GoalDetailView`'s child Subject-plan cards (`frontend/app/collections/[id]/collection-detail-page-client.tsx:1201-1217`) have shown `overallReadinessPercentage` plus a progress bar since the original Goal → Subject hierarchy feature (2026-06-30) — a mastery-percentage display that `AGENTS.md:142` and `docs/features/collections.md:481`'s "no-mastery rule" only documented as excepted for the dedicated `/progress?collectionId={id}` route. Found while scoping the due-color item below; not a behavior change, corrects the docs to name this second, already-shipped exception explicitly.
- **Overdue color-warning on child Subject-plan cards (frontend only).** `child.dueConcepts` is already rendered as plain text in the same card's stats line (`collection-detail-page-client.tsx:1218-1220`) with no color coding. When `dueConcepts > 0`, that segment renders in the existing amber-warning color already used elsewhere in this file for `getNoteExecutionStatus`'s "Needs Study Pack" state; `0` stays muted. Presence-based only, not a magnitude threshold — avoids inventing a new threshold system.

Anti-drift: presentation + doc-only — no new backend field, no new threshold constant, no change to how `dueConcepts` or `overallReadinessPercentage` are computed; the due-color treatment is additive to the card already carrying a mastery signal, not a departure into new "monitoring dashboard" territory on a previously metadata-only surface.

### Shipped

- **Doc fix: formalized the Goal-detail readiness exception.** `AGENTS.md:142` and `docs/features/collections.md:481` now name `GoalDetailView`'s child Subject-plan cards as a second, deliberate exception to the no-mastery rule — the `overallReadinessPercentage`/progress-bar display has been live there since the original Goal → Subject hierarchy feature, just never documented as sanctioned.
- **Overdue color-warning on child Subject-plan cards.** `collection-detail-page-client.tsx`'s `GoalDetailView` now renders the `dueConcepts` segment of each child card's stats line in the existing amber-warning color (`text-amber-700 dark:text-amber-300`) when `dueConcepts > 0`; `0` stays muted. Presence-based only, matching precedent from this file's own `getNoteExecutionStatus`. Verified in both light and dark mode via a real render.

## v0.66.0 - Challenge Quiz Result Clarity

**Status: Released**

Theme: consolidate the standard Challenge Quiz result screen's score/Score Summary/Concept Breakdown into a single coherent outcome hierarchy, closing the gap flagged as a Known Limitation while signing off `v0.65.0`. Reclaims the `v0.66.0` minor-version slot from `Explore Convergence` (renumbered to `v0.67.0`), since this item is small, ungated, and ready now, while Explore's Diagnostic Read gate remains unmet (due after 2026-08-06) — the seventh time a slot has changed hands this way, following `v0.60.0`, `v0.61.0`, `v0.62.0`, `v0.63.0`, `v0.64.0`, and `v0.65.0`.

### Planned Scope

- **Challenge Quiz outcome-area consolidation (frontend only).** Standard Challenge Quiz mode's result branch (`frontend/app/study-packs/[id]/challenge-quiz/page.tsx`, ~line 2548-2639) currently stacks three separate visual blocks that partially duplicate each other: a raw score `<div>` (percentage, correct count, duration, message, performance badge), a separate "Score Summary" `Card` re-displaying the same Correct/Answered/Percentage numbers in a grid, and a separate "Concept Breakdown" `Card`. Board Exam Mode's own result branch in the same file (~line 2331-2379) already solved this with the shared `ScoreReveal` component (`frontend/components/exam-mode/score-reveal.tsx`) plus one Concept Breakdown card — standard mode should converge on the same pattern rather than keep its own separate, more duplicated layout.
- **`ScoreReveal` needs a third tone.** Currently a closed `tone: "long-exam" | "board-exam"` union; standard Challenge Quiz mode needs its own tone value (e.g. `"challenge-quiz"`) rather than reusing `"board-exam"`'s styling by coincidence.
- **Resolved dual-metric design:** standard mode has a case Board Exam Mode doesn't — when a session has unanswered questions, it shows both "Answered Accuracy" and "Overall Completion Score." `ScoreReveal` gains an optional subordinate `secondaryMetric` slot for the completion score, while Answered Accuracy remains the primary reveal; existing Board Exam and Long Exam calls omit the slot and retain their existing output.

Anti-drift: presentation-only — no score calculation, concept-breakdown data, or session logic changes; Quick Review and Adaptive Practice result screens are not in scope (this Known Limitation named Challenge Quiz specifically); Board Exam Mode's already-clean branch is the reference pattern to converge toward, not to modify.

### Shipped

- **Challenge Quiz outcome-area consolidation (frontend only).** Standard Challenge Quiz results now use the shared `ScoreReveal` hierarchy for the primary score, correct/answered count, duration, and performance pill, followed by the existing Concept Breakdown card; the duplicated raw score block and separate Score Summary card are removed. Sessions with unanswered questions keep both required metrics in one outcome block through an optional subordinate `secondaryMetric` for Overall Completion Score, with the explanatory copy now positioned directly under the reveal instead of separated by the result message. `ScoreReveal` now has a dedicated `challenge-quiz` tone, while Board Exam and Long Exam omit the optional slot and retain their previous DOM/classes. As a result of adopting the shared component, the performance pill picks up `ScoreReveal`'s existing outline-only style (already live on Board Exam Mode) in place of Challenge Quiz's own filled/tinted badge — a deliberate choice, since filled hue badges read poorly in dark mode; "Good" now renders as a neutral outline like the other non-extreme levels instead of filled blue.

## v0.65.0 - Study Effectiveness Polish

**Status: Released**

Theme: close out the remaining, still-valid candidates from the 2026-07-22 Study Effectiveness/UI Polish/Pricing Fit consultation — surfacing what a Study Pack actually contains before a learner commits, cleaning up the card stack that's accreted on Review Set Detail and the quiz result screens across many separate releases, making the collapsed Companion discoverable, and tagging why Adaptive Practice picked a given question. Reclaims the `v0.65.0` minor-version slot from `Explore Convergence` (renumbered to `v0.66.0`), since this item is small, ungated, and ready now, while Explore's Diagnostic Read gate remains unmet (due after 2026-08-06) — the sixth time a slot has changed hands this way, following `v0.60.0`, `v0.61.0`, `v0.62.0`, `v0.63.0`, and `v0.64.0`.

### Planned Scope

- **Study Pack scope surfacing (backend + frontend).** Concept count, quiz length, and rough review time on the Summary tab and on library note cards, before a learner commits to a session. `StudyPackEntity.keyConcepts`/`.quiz` already carry this data for the Summary tab; the note-list response DTO needs a small addition to carry counts for the card view too.
- **Card-accretion layout pass on Review Set Detail and quiz result screens (frontend only, Codex).** Presentation-only reorder/consolidation across the detail page and all 3 result-screen variants (Quick Review, Challenge Quiz, Adaptive Practice) — no logic change. The stack has grown since the original 2026-07-22 audit (Ask Companion panel, GoalNudgeCard, TwiceMissedAskCompanionCard all added in v0.63.0).
- **Collapsed-Companion teaser (frontend only).** A one-line overview excerpt in `CompanionDisplayCard`'s collapsed state, so the feature isn't literally invisible until a learner clicks the toggle — without reversing the deliberate collapse-by-default decision.
- **Adaptive Practice per-question rationale tag (backend + frontend, Codex).** New selection-provenance metadata threaded from `QuickReviewAdaptivePracticeService`'s weak/due-concept selection logic into a parallel per-question session-state array (not `QuizItem`), rendered during the session (e.g. "Reviewing: Ohm's Law — missed last time").

Anti-drift: none of these four touch `EXAM_MODES.md`'s locked no-mid-exam-coaching rule — the rationale tag is static deterministic metadata, not LLM-driven, so it doesn't cross into "interactive AI"; the layout pass is presentation-only, no card removed or logic changed; Companion's collapse-by-default decision (Coach vs. Companion doctrine) stays, only the collapsed-state emptiness is fixed. Two other candidates from the same original consultation are already resolved and explicitly out of scope: Note Detail tab order (locked decision, nudge already shipped) and Key Concepts readiness sort (already shipped) — corrected in `docs/product/ROADMAP.md`'s Backlog Index as part of this kickoff.

### Shipped

- **Collapsed-Companion teaser (frontend only).** `CompanionDisplayCard` now shows a single-line plain-text excerpt (first non-empty of Overview / Study Strategy / Common Mistakes, markdown stripped, CSS line-clamped) under the toggle while collapsed, instead of rendering nothing until a learner clicks "View Full Guide." The collapse-by-default decision is unchanged; a Companion with only FAQ/Resources populated still shows no teaser.
- **Study Pack scope surfacing (backend + frontend).** Private Note Detail's Summary tab and private Library cards now show ready-pack concept count, quiz length, and a deterministic rough review-time estimate before a learner starts. Library reuses its existing `quizCount` and now receives nullable `keyConceptCount`; draft, generating, failed, and empty-pack states show no misleading zero-value scope row. Public/discovery cards remain unchanged.
- **Result and Review Set Detail layout pass (frontend only).** Goal detail now keeps Today’s Focus, readiness, and post-adopt guidance as one tight study-decision cluster, followed by a tight Companion/Q&A cluster. Quick Review, Challenge Quiz, Adaptive Practice, and Board Exam Mode's own result branch now consolidate their existing next-step/pacing cards and Companion-adjacent cards into shared visual groups; every action, conditional, and data fetch is unchanged. Board Exam Mode's Companion group only ever contains its existing Companion excerpt card (no twice-missed CTA there, unchanged from before this pass). Long Exam's forfeit-only flow remains untouched; Board Exam Mode's feedback-free *in-session* view (not its result screen) is also unaffected. **A real-browser visual audit (production CSS, mocked API data) found and this release fixed two presentation bugs the RTL/tsc/lint pass couldn't catch:** `ResultGuidanceGroup` used `<Card className="... p-0">` to zero out padding, but this codebase's `cn()` is a plain string join (no `tailwind-merge`), so `Card`'s hardcoded `p-6` and the passed `p-0` both landed in the DOM and Tailwind's compiled stylesheet order — not DOM order — picked the winner, leaving every group inset by a full card's padding on top of its own; fixed by dropping `Card` for a plain div replicating its other base styles. Separately, Goal Detail's new Companion-guidance `<section>` wrapper rendered unconditionally even when both children inside it were null (no renderable Companion), leaving an empty box that still consumed a slot in the parent's spacing rhythm — fixed by gating the whole section on the same `hasRenderableCompanionContent` check its children already used individually.
- **Adaptive Practice per-question rationale tag (backend + frontend).** Active questions now identify why their concept was targeted with distinct due-only, weak-only, or combined copy. `QuickReviewAdaptivePracticeService` captures that provenance before due and previously-missed concepts merge, stores a nullable reason array beside the quiz in session JSONB, and returns the same index-aligned array on both start and resume. Unmatched LLM-generated concepts stay untagged rather than receiving a guessed rationale; `QuizItem`, generation prompts, scoring, and every other quiz mode remain unchanged.

### Known Limitations

- **Challenge Quiz's outcome area (score, Score Summary, per-metric cards, Concept Breakdown) still reads as an accreted card stack** — a real-browser visual audit flagged this as the reason Challenge Quiz's result screen is the weakest of the three (Adaptive Practice's outcome hierarchy was rated strongest). This is a materially larger redesign than this release's scope: the original Codex prompt for this layout pass explicitly named only the post-session guidance cards (`PostSessionNextStep`/`GoalNudgeCard`/`WeeklyPacingEchoCard` and `CompanionResultBridgeCard`/`TwiceMissedAskCompanionCard`), not the score/outcome area above them. Merging those into one outcome block with a compact metric grid needs its own scoping pass and Codex prompt, not an ad-hoc expansion of this one. Not fixed this release.
- **Adaptive Practice's rationale pill row has not had a real-browser visual check.** The longest single-reason string ("missed last time and due for review") is 62 characters in a `rounded-full` pill, and a matching-group question can render 2-3 distinct pills in one `flex-wrap` row — neither case is covered by the RTL tests added this release (all use a single short concept). Code-level review confirmed the row renders only in the active-question branch, never on the result screen. The owner opted to eyeball it directly rather than route through a separate visual-review pass; if it turns out to wrap badly on narrow viewports, that's a follow-up fix, not a re-open of this release.

## v0.64.0 - Add to Review Set

**Status: Released**

Theme: close a genuine capability gap surfaced while discussing v0.63.0's Companion scoping question — a user reading a Note who wants it organized into a Review Set has no way to do that from Note Detail today. Reclaims the `v0.64.0` minor-version slot from `Explore Convergence` (renumbered to `v0.65.0`), since this item is small, ungated, and ready now, while Explore's Diagnostic Read gate remains unmet — the fifth time a slot has changed hands this way, following `v0.60.0`, `v0.61.0`, `v0.62.0`, and `v0.63.0`.

### Planned Scope

- **"Add to {Review Set}" on Note Detail (frontend only).** Extracts the existing, already-built `AddImportedDraftsModal` (`frontend/components/notes/bulk-import-page-client.tsx`) into a shared component and mounts it from Note Detail with the single viewed note, giving a user-initiated way to add an existing note to an existing or new collection without leaving the page. No backend changes — reuses `listCollections`, `addCollectionItems`, and `createCollection` as-is.
